              (Fin.fintype (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst)) :=
fun {F} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F]
    [inst_2 :
      @CharP.{u_1} F
        (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{u_1} F

    m =>
  @Subgroup.closure.{u_1}
    (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F

    (@Union.union.{u_1}
      (Set.{u_1}
        (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F

        (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F

        (Set.{u_1}

  @Option.casesOn.{u_2, u_1} (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F) (fun x => motive x) x (h_1 Unit.unit) fun val =>

      h_2 fst snd

          @Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst) →
            Prop :=
fun {F} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F]
    [inst_2 :
      @CharP.{u_1} F
        (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{u_1} F
          (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{u_1} F (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_1} F (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_1} F inst))))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))]
    m g =>
  Or
    (∃ a b t,
      @Eq.{u_1 + 1}
        (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst))
        g (@Atlas.SuzukiFamily.shortForm.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m a b t))
    (∃ a b t c d,
      @Eq.{u_1 + 1}
        (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F

DEFINITION Atlas.Symplectic.fieldEquivSp SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Symplectic.FieldTransport
def Atlas.Symplectic.fieldEquivSp.{u_1, u_2} : {n : Nat} →
  {F : Type u_1} →
    {K : Type u_2} →
      [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
        [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
          @RingEquiv.{u_1, u_2} F K
              (@Distrib.toMul.{u_1} F
                (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
              (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
                (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
              (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_1} F
                (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
              (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
                (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))) →
            @MulEquiv.{u_1, u_2} (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} n F (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst))
              (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_2} n K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
              (@Submonoid.mul.{u_1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_1} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) F)
                (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_1}
                  (Matrix.{0, 0, u_1} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) F)
                  (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_1}
                    (Matrix.{0, 0, u_1} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) F)
                    (@Matrix.semiring.{u_1, 0} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) F
                      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst)))
                      (@instFintypeSum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (Fin.fintype n)) fun a b =>
                      @instDecidableEqSum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) a b)))
                (@Matrix.symplecticGroup.{0, u_1} (Fin n) F (instDecidableEqFin n) (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst)
                  (Fin.fintype n)))
              (@Submonoid.mul.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) K)
                (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                  (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) K)
                  (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2}
                    (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) K)
                    (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) K
                      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                      (@instFintypeSum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (Fin.fintype n)) fun a b =>
                      @instDecidableEqSum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) a b)))
                (@Matrix.symplecticGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) K (instDecidableEqFin n) (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                  (Fin.fintype n))) :=
fun {n} {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] e =>
  @MulEquiv.mk.{u_1, u_2} (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} n F (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst))
    (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_2} n K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
    (@Submonoid.mul.{u_1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_1} (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) (Sum.{0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n)) F)
      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_1}

              (@EquivLike.toFunLike.{max (u_1 + 1) (u_2 + 1), u_2 + 1, u_1 + 1}
                (@RingEquiv.{u_2, u_1} K F
                  (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                  (@Distrib.toMul.{u_1} F
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                  (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                  (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_1} F
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))))))
                K F
                (@RingEquiv.instEquivLike.{u_2, u_1} K F
                  (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                  (@Distrib.toMul.{u_1} F
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                  (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                  (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_1} F
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))))
              (@RingEquiv.symm.{u_1, u_2} F K
                (@Distrib.toMul.{u_1} F
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_1} F
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_1} F
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                e)))
          ⋯)
      ⋯ ⋯)
    ⋯

DEFINITION Atlas.SuzukiFamily.embedding SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.SuzukiFamily.Model
def Atlas.SuzukiFamily.embedding.{u_1} : {F : Type u_1} →
  [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
    [inst_1 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
      [inst_2 :

        (m : Nat) →
          @MonoidHom.{u_1, u_1} (@Atlas.SuzukiFamily.Group.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m)
            (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst))
            (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_1} (@Atlas.SuzukiFamily.Group.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m)
              (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_1} (@Atlas.SuzukiFamily.Group.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m)
                (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_1} (@Atlas.SuzukiFamily.Group.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m)
                  (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_1} (@Atlas.SuzukiFamily.Group.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m)
                    (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_1}
                      (@Atlas.Symplectic.Sp.{u_1} (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) F

                (Prod.{u_1, u_1}
                  (@Units.{u_1} F
                    (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_1} F
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                  (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F))
                x snd)))
        fst fun fst snd_1 =>
        @Prod.casesOn.{u_2, u_1, u_1}
          (@Units.{u_1} F
            (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_1} F
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
          (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F)
          (fun x =>
            motive
              (@Sum.inr.{u_1, u_1}
                (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F
                  (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F
                    (@Units.{u_1} F
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_1} F
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))))
                (Prod.{u_1, u_1} (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F)
                  (Prod.{u_1, u_1}
                    (@Units.{u_1} F
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_1} F
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                    (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F)))
                (@Prod.mk.{u_1, u_1} (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F)
                  (Prod.{u_1, u_1}
                    (@Units.{u_1} F
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_1} F
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                    (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F))
                  (@Prod.mk.{u_1, u_1} F F fst snd_1) x)))
          snd fun fst_1 snd =>
          @Prod.casesOn.{u_2, u_1, u_1} F F
            (fun x =>
              motive
                (@Sum.inr.{u_1, u_1}
                  (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F

                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
                      (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F)))
                  (@Prod.mk.{u_1, u_1} (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F)
                    (Prod.{u_1, u_1}

                      (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F F) fst_1 x))))
            snd fun fst_2 snd => h_2 fst snd_1 fst_1 fst_2 snd

      @CharP.{u_1} F
        (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{u_1} F

        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))]
    m hcard =>
  @Equiv.ofBijective.{u_1 + 1, u_1 + 1}
    (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F
      (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F

    (↥(@Atlas.SuzukiFamily.borel.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m hcard))
    (fun p =>
      @Atlas.SuzukiFamily.shortInBorel.{u_1} F inst inst_1 inst_2 m hcard
        (@Prod.fst.{u_1, u_1} F
          (Prod.{u_1, u_1} F

          p)
        (@Prod.fst.{u_1, u_1} F

                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))))))
            p))
        (@Prod.snd.{u_1, u_1} F
